  │ 647 + def run_all_tests() -> Tuple[int, int, List[ProofObject]]:       │
  │ 648 +     """Run all Commonwealth tests.                               │
  │ 649 +                                                                  │
  │ 650 +     Returns:                                                     │
  │ 651 +         (passed, total, proofs)                                  │

  │ 653 +     tests = [                                                    │
  │ 654 +         test_sovereign_grant_capability,                         │

  │ 656 +         test_sovereign_declare_sabbath,                          │
  │ 657 +         test_steward_capability_check,                           │
  │ 658 +         test_steward_execute_within_capabilities,                │

  │ 660 +         test_steward_witness_action,                             │
  │ 661 +         test_sabbath_completion_checking,                        │
  │ 662 +         test_sabbath_halt_declaration,                           │
  │ 663 +         test_sabbath_halt_blocked_on_incomplete,                 │
  │ 664 +         test_dispute_file_violation,                             │
  │ 665 +         test_dispute_resolve,                                    │
  │ 666 +         test_dispute_list_pending,                               │
  │ 667 +         test_dispute_auto_file_on_violation,                     │
  │ 668 +         test_system_state_completion_ratio,                      │
  │ 669 +         test_scope_immutability,                                 │
  │ 670 +         test_grant_record_proof,                                 │
  │ 671 +     ]                                                            │
  │ 672 +                                                                  │
  │ 673 +     passed = 0                                                   │
  │ 674 +     proofs = []                                                  │
  │ 675 +                                                                  │
  │ 676 +     for test in tests:                                           │
  │ 677 +         try:                                                     │
  │ 678 +             success, proof = test()                              │
  │ 679 +             if success and proof.is_valid():                     │
  │ 680 +                 passed += 1                                      │
  │ 681 +                 proofs.append(proof)                             │
  │ 682 +             else:                                                │
  │ 683 +                 proofs.append(ProofObject(                       │
  │ 684 +                     rule="TestFailed",                           │
  │ 685 +                     premises=[f"test={test.__name__}"],          │
  │ 686 +                     conclusion="test failed or invalid proof"    │
  │ 687 +                 ))                                               │
  │ 688 +         except Exception as e:                                   │
  │ 689 +             proofs.append(ProofObject(                           │
  │ 690 +                 rule="TestError",                                │
  │ 691 +                 premises=[f"test={test.__name__}", f"error={str… │
  │ 692 +                 conclusion="test raised exception"               │
  │ 693 +             ))                                                   │
  │ 694 +                                                                  │
  │ 695 +     return passed, len(tests), proofs                            │
  │ 696 +                                                                  │
  │ 697 +                                                                  │
  │ 698 + if __name__ == "__main__":                                       │
  │ 699 +     print("=" * 70)                                              │
  │ 700 +     print("YESHUA COMMONWEALTH KERNEL TESTS")                    │
  │ 701 +     print("=" * 70)                                              │
  │ 702 +     print()                                                      │
  │ 703 +                                                                  │
  │ 704 +     passed, total, proofs = run_all_tests()                      │
  │ 705 +                                                                  │
  │ 706 +     print(f"Tests: {passed}/{total} passed")                     │
  │ 707 +     print()                                                      │
  │ 708 +                                                                  │
  │ 709 +     if passed == total:                                          │
  │ 710 +         print("✅ All Commonwealth kernel tests passed!")        │
  │ 711 +     else:                                                        │
  │ 712 +         print(f"❌ {total - passed} test(s) failed")             │
  │ 713 +         print()                                                  │
  │ 714 +         for proof in proofs:                                     │
  │ 715 +             if "Failed" in proof.rule or "Error" in proof.rule:  │
  │ 716 +                 print(f"  - {proof.conclusion}")                 │
  │ 717 +                                                                  │
  │ 718 +     print()                                                      │
  │ 719 +     print("=" * 70)                                              │

  │ 205 +     def request_consent(                                         │

  │ 275 +                                                                  │

  │ 277 +             grant_id=grant_id,                                   │

  │ 280 +             grantee_id=request.sender_id,                        │
  │ 281 +             scope=scope,                                         │

  │ 284 +             expires_at=expires_at,                               │

  │ 286 +                                                                  │

  │ 288 +                                                                  │
  │ 289 +         # Create channel                                         │
  │ 290 +         channel_id = f"CHAN_{request.sender_id}_{request.receiv… │
  │ 291 +         channel = ConsentChannel(                                │
  │ 292 +             channel_id=channel_id,                               │
  │ 293 +             sender_id=request.sender_id,                         │

  │ 302 +                 conclusion="failed: data too small"              │
  │ 303 +             )                                                    │
  │ 304 +                                                                  │
  │ 305 +         # Check magic number (would validate 0xD00DFEED)         │
  │ 306 +         magic = int.from_bytes(dtb_data[:4], 'big')              │
  │ 307 +                                                                  │
  │ 308 +         tree = DeviceTree()                                      │
  │ 309 +                                                                  │
  │ 310 +         proof = ProofObject(                                     │

  │ 196 +     pts: Dict[Tuple[int, int, int], PageTable] = field(default_… │
  │ 197 +                                                                  │
  │ 198 +     def walk(                                                    │
  │ 199 +         self,                                                    │
  │ 200 +         virtual_address: Fraction                                │
  │ 201 +     ) -> Tuple[Optional[PageTableEntry], List[ProofObject]]:     │
  │ 202 +         """Walk page tables for virtual address.                 │
  │ 203 +                                                                  │
  │ 204 +         Args:                                                    │
  │ 205 +             virtual_address: Virtual address to translate        │
  │ 206 +                                                                  │
  │ 207 +         Returns:                                                 │
  │ 208 +             (pte, proofs) — pte is None if not present, proofs … │
  │ 209 +         """                                                      │
  │ 210 +         proofs = []                                              │
  │ 211 +                                                                  │
  │ 212 +         # Extract indices from virtual address                   │
  │ 213 +         addr_int = int(virtual_address)                          │
  │ 214 +         pml4_idx = (addr_int >> 39) & 0x1FF                      │
  │ 215 +         pdpt_idx = (addr_int >> 30) & 0x1FF                      │
  │ 216 +         pd_idx = (addr_int >> 21) & 0x1FF                        │
  │ 217 +         pt_idx = (addr_int >> 12) & 0x1FF                        │
  │ 218 +                                                                  │

  │ 220 +         pml4_entry, proof = self.pml4.get_entry(pml4_idx)        │
  │ 221 +         proofs.append(proof)                                     │
  │ 222 +                                                                  │
  │ 223 +         if pml4_entry is None or not pml4_entry.is_present():    │
  │ 224 +             return None, proofs                                  │
  │ 225 +                                                                  │
  │ 226 +         # Walk PDPT                                              │
  │ 227 +         pdpt = self.pdpts.get(pml4_idx)                          │
  │ 228 +         if pdpt is None:                                         │
  │ 229 +             return None, proofs + [ProofObject(                  │

  │ 231 +                 premises=[f"pdpt_missing={pml4_idx}"],           │
  │ 232 +                 conclusion="PDPT not present"                    │
  │ 233 +             )]                                                   │
  │ 234 +                                                                  │

  │ 236 +         proofs.append(proof)                                     │
  │ 237 +                                                                  │
  │ 238 +         if pdpt_entry is None or not pdpt_entry.is_present():    │
  │ 239 +             return None, proofs                                  │
  │ 240 +                                                                  │
  │ 241 +         # Check for 1GB huge page                                │
  │ 242 +         if pdpt_entry.is_huge():                                 │
  │ 243 +             return pdpt_entry, proofs                            │
  │ 244 +                                                                  │
  │ 245 +         # Walk PD                                                │
  │ 246 +         pd = self.pds.get((pml4_idx, pdpt_idx))                  │
  │ 247 +         if pd is None:                                           │

  │ 249 +                 rule="PageWalk",                                 │
  │ 250 +                 premises=[f"pd_missing=({pml4_idx},{pdpt_idx})"… │
  │ 251 +                 conclusion="PD not present"                      │
  │ 252 +             )]                                                   │
  │ 253 +                                                                  │
  │ 254 +         pd_entry, proof = pd.get_entry(pd_idx)                   │
  │ 255 +         proofs.append(proof)                                     │
  │ 256 +                                                                  │
  │ 257 +         if pd_entry is None or not pd_entry.is_present():        │
  │ 258 +             return None, proofs                                  │
  │ 259 +                                                                  │
  │ 260 +         # Check for 2MB huge page                                │
  │ 261 +         if pd_entry.is_huge():                                   │
  │ 262 +             return pd_entry, proofs                              │
  │ 263 +                                                                  │
  │ 264 +         # Walk PT                                                │
  │ 265 +         pt = self.pts.get((pml4_idx, pdpt_idx, pd_idx))          │
  │ 266 +         if pt is None:                                           │

  │ 268 +                 rule="PageWalk",                                 │

  │ 271 +             )]                                                   │
  │ 272 +                                                                  │
  │ 273 +         pt_entry, proof = pt.get_entry(pt_idx)                   │
  │ 274 +         proofs.append(proof)                                     │
  │ 275 +                                                                  │
  │ 276 +         return pt_entry, proofs                                  │
  │ 277 +                                                                  │
  │ 278 +     def map_page(                                                │
  │ 279 +         self,                                                    │
  │ 280 +         virtual_address: Fraction,                               │
  │ 281 +         physical_address: Fraction,                              │
  │ 282 +         writable: bool = True,                                   │
  │ 283 +         user: bool = True,                                       │
  │ 284 +         executable: bool = True,                                 │
  │ 285 +     ) -> Tuple[bool, List[ProofObject]]:                         │
  │ 286 +         """Map a virtual page to a physical page.                │
  │ 287 +                                                                  │
  │ 288 +         Creates intermediate tables as needed.                   │
  │ 289 +         """                                                      │
  │ 290 +         proofs = []                                              │
  │ 291 +                                                                  │
  │ 292 +         # Extract indices                                        │
  │ 293 +         addr_int = int(virtual_address)                          │
  │ 294 +         pml4_idx = (addr_int >> 39) & 0x1FF                      │
  │ 295 +         pdpt_idx = (addr_int >> 30) & 0x1FF                      │
  │ 296 +         pd_idx = (addr_int >> 21) & 0x1FF                        │
  │ 297 +         pt_idx = (addr_int >> 12) & 0x1FF                        │
  │ 298 +                                                                  │
  │ 299 +         # Ensure PDPT exists                                     │
  │ 300 +         if pml4_idx not in self.pdpts:                           │
  │ 301 +             self.pdpts[pml4_idx] = PageTable(level=2)            │
  │ 302 +             # Link in PML4                                       │
  │ 303 +             pdpt_phys = Fraction(0x1000 * (pml4_idx + 1))  # Pl… │
  │ 304 +             self.pml4.set_entry(pml4_idx, PageTableEntry(        │
  │ 305 +                 physical_address=pdpt_phys,                      │
  │ 306 +                 flags=0x7  # Present, writable, user             │
  │ 307 +             ))                                                   │
  │ 308 +                                                                  │
  │ 309 +         # Ensure PD exists                                       │
  │ 310 +         if (pml4_idx, pdpt_idx) not in self.pds:                 │
  │ 311 +             self.pds[(pml4_idx, pdpt_idx)] = PageTable(level=1)  │
  │ 312 +                                                                  │
  │ 313 +         # Ensure PT exists                                       │
  │ 314 +         if (pml4_idx, pdpt_idx, pd_idx) not in self.pts:         │
  │ 315 +             self.pts[(pml4_idx, pdpt_idx, pd_idx)] = PageTable(… │
  │ 316 +                                                                  │
  │ 317 +         # Create PTE                                             │
  │ 318 +         flags = (1 << PagePermission.PRESENT.value)              │
  │ 319 +         if writable:                                             │
  │ 320 +             flags |= (1 << PagePermission.WRITABLE.value)        │
  │ 321 +         if user:                                                 │
  │ 322 +             flags |= (1 << PagePermission.USER.value)            │
  │ 323 +         if not executable:                                       │
  │ 324 +             flags |= (1 << PagePermission.NX.value)              │
  │ 325 +                                                                  │
  │ 326 +         pte = PageTableEntry(                                    │
  │ 327 +             physical_address=physical_address,                   │
  │ 328 +             flags=flags                                          │
  │ 329 +         )                                                        │
  │ 330 +                                                                  │
  │ 331 +         # Set in PT                                              │
  │ 332 +         pt = self.pts[(pml4_idx, pdpt_idx, pd_idx)]              │
  │ 333 +         success, proof = pt.set_entry(pt_idx, pte)               │
  │ 334 +         proofs.append(proof)                                     │
  │ 335 +                                                                  │
  │ 336 +         return success, proofs                                   │
  │ 337 +                                                                  │
  │ 338 +     def unmap_page(self, virtual_address: Fraction) -> Tuple[bo… │
  │ 339 +         """Unmap a virtual page."""                              │
  │ 340 +         addr_int = int(virtual_address)                          │
  │ 341 +         pml4_idx = (addr_int >> 39) & 0x1FF                      │
  │ 342 +         pdpt_idx = (addr_int >> 30) & 0x1FF                      │
  │ 343 +         pd_idx = (addr_int >> 21) & 0x1FF                        │
  │ 344 +         pt_idx = (addr_int >> 12) & 0x1FF                        │
  │ 345 +                                                                  │
  │ 346 +         pt = self.pts.get((pml4_idx, pdpt_idx, pd_idx))          │
  │ 347 +         if pt is None:                                           │

  │ 170 +             new_physical_page: New physical page to copy into    │
  │ 171 +                                                                  │
  │ 172 +         Returns:                                                 │
  │ 173 +             (new_page, proof) — new_page is None if not a COW f… │
  │ 174 +         """                                                      │
  │ 175 +         virtual_page = Fraction(int(virtual_address) >> 12)      │
  │ 176 +                                                                  │
  │ 177 +         # Find the COW page                                      │
  │ 178 +         for phys, page in list(self.pages.items()):              │
  │ 179 +             if process_id in page.virtual_pages:                 │
  │ 180 +                 proc_virt = page.virtual_pages[process_id]       │
  │ 181 +                 if proc_virt == virtual_page:                    │

  │ 183 +                     if page.reference_count == 1:                │

  │ 186 +                             physical_page=phys,                  │
  │ 187 +                             virtual_pages={process_id: virtual_… │
  │ 188 +                             reference_count=1,                   │
  │ 189 +                             status=COWStatus.PRIVATE,            │
  │ 190 +                             original_writable=page.original_wri… │
  │ 191 +                         )                                        │
  │ 192 +                         self.pages[phys] = new_page              │
  │ 193 +                                                                  │

  │ 195 +                             rule="COWWriteFault",                │

  │ 198 +                                 f"va=0x{int(virtual_address):x}… │

  │ 200 +                             conclusion="made writable (sole ref… │

  │ 202 +                                                                  │
  │ 203 +                     # Multiple references — need to copy         │
  │ 204 +                     # Decrement reference on old page            │
  │ 205 +                     old_virtual_pages = dict(page.virtual_pages) │
  │ 206 +                     del old_virtual_pages[process_id]            │
  │ 207 +                                                                  │

  │ 209 +                         physical_page=phys,                      │
  │ 210 +                         virtual_pages=old_virtual_pages,         │
  │ 211 +                         reference_count=page.reference_count - … │
  │ 212 +                         status=COWStatus.SHARED,                 │
  │ 213 +                         original_writable=page.original_writable │
  │ 214 +                     )                                            │
  │ 215 +                     self.pages[phys] = updated_old               │
  │ 216 +                                                                  │
  │ 217 +                     # Create new private page                    │
  │ 218 +                     new_page = COWPage(                          │
  │ 219 +                         physical_page=new_physical_page,         │
  │ 220 +                         virtual_pages={process_id: virtual_page… │
  │ 221 +                         reference_count=1,                       │
  │ 222 +                         status=COWStatus.PRIVATE,                │
  │ 223 +                         original_writable=True                   │
  │ 224 +                     )                                            │
  │ 225 +                     self.pages[new_physical_page] = new_page     │
  │ 226 +                                                                  │
  │ 227 +                     self.total_copied_pages += 1                 │
  │ 228 +                                                                  │
  │ 229 +                     return new_page, ProofObject(                │
  │ 230 +                         rule="COWWriteFault",                    │
  │ 231 +                         premises=[                               │
  │ 232 +                             f"process={process_id}",             │
  │ 233 +                             f"va=0x{int(virtual_address):x}",    │
  │ 234 +                             f"old_phys=0x{int(phys):x}",         │
  │ 235 +                             f"new_phys=0x{int(new_physical_page… │

  │ 239 +                                                                  │

  │ 241 +             rule="COWWriteFault",                                │

  │ 243 +             conclusion="not a COW page"                          │
  │ 244 +         )                                                        │
  │ 245 +                                                                  │
  │ 246 +     def release_pages(                                           │
  │ 247 +         self,                                                    │
  │ 248 +         process_id: str,                                         │
  │ 249 +         virtual_pages: List[Fraction]                            │
  │ 250 +     ) -> Tuple[int, ProofObject]:                                │
  │ 251 +         """Release COW pages when process exits or munmaps.      │
  │ 252 +                                                                  │
  │ 253 +         Args:                                                    │
  │ 254 +             process_id: Process releasing pages                  │
  │ 255 +             virtual_pages: Virtual pages being released          │
  │ 256 +                                                                  │
  │ 257 +         Returns:                                                 │
  │ 258 +             (freed_pages, proof)                                 │
  │ 259 +         """                                                      │
  │ 260 +         freed = 0                                                │
  │ 261 +                                                                  │
  │ 262 +         for virt in virtual_pages:                               │
  │ 263 +             for phys, page in list(self.pages.items()):          │
  │ 264 +                 if (process_id in page.virtual_pages and         │
  │ 265 +                     page.virtual_pages[process_id] == virt):     │
  │ 266 +                                                                  │
  │ 267 +                     if page.reference_count == 1:                │
  │ 268 +                         # Last reference — can free              │
  │ 269 +                         del self.pages[phys]                     │
  │ 270 +                         freed += 1                               │
  │ 271 +                     else:                                        │
  │ 272 +                         # Decrement reference                    │
  │ 273 +                         new_virtual_pages = dict(page.virtual_p… │
  │ 274 +                         del new_virtual_pages[process_id]        │
  │ 275 +                                                                  │
  │ 276 +                         new_page = COWPage(                      │
  │ 277 +                             physical_page=phys,                  │
  │ 278 +                             virtual_pages=new_virtual_pages,     │
  │ 279 +                             reference_count=page.reference_coun… │
  │ 280 +                             status=page.status,                  │
  │ 281 +                             original_writable=page.original_wri… │
  │ 282 +                         )                                        │
  │ 283 +                         self.pages[phys] = new_page              │
  │ 284 +                                                                  │
  │ 285 +                     break                                        │
  │ 286 +                                                                  │
  │ 287 +         return freed, ProofObject(                               │
  │ 288 +             rule="COWRelease",                                   │
  │ 289 +             premises=[                                           │
  │ 290 +                 f"process={process_id}",                         │
  │ 291 +                 f"released={len(virtual_pages)}",                │
  │ 292 +                 f"freed={freed}",                                │
  │ 293 +             ],                                                   │
  │ 294 +             conclusion="pages released"                          │
  │ 295 +         )                                                        │
  │ 296 +                                                                  │
  │ 297 +     def get_stats(self) -> Tuple[Dict[str, int], ProofObject]:   │
  │ 298 +         """Get COW statistics."""                                │
  │ 299 +         shared = sum(1 for p in self.pages.values() if p.is_sha… │
  │ 300 +         private = sum(1 for p in self.pages.values() if not p.i… │
  │ 301 +                                                                  │
  │ 302 +         stats = {                                                │
  │ 303 +             "tracked_pages": len(self.pages),                    │
  │ 304 +             "shared_pages": shared,                              │
  │ 305 +             "private_pages": private,                            │
  │ 306 +             "total_shared_creations": self.total_shared_pages,   │
  │ 307 +             "total_copies": self.total_copied_pages,             │
  │ 308 +         }                                                        │
  │ 309 +                                                                  │
  │ 310 +         proof = ProofObject(                                     │
  │ 311 +             rule="COWStats",                                     │
  │ 312 +             premises=[f"tracked={stats['tracked_pages']}"],      │
  │ 313 +             conclusion="stats retrieved"                         │
  │ 314 +         )                                                        │
  │ 315 +                                                                  │
  │ 316 +         return stats, proof                                      │

  │  44 +                                                                  │
  │  45 +     def verify_content(self, content: bytes) -> Tuple[bool, Pro… │
  │  46 +         """Verify content matches hash."""                       │
  │  47 +         computed = hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()           │
  │  48 +         valid = computed == self.content_hash                    │
  │  49 +                                                                  │
  │  50 +         return valid, ProofObject(                               │
  │  51 +             rule="InodeVerifyContent",                           │
  │  52 +             premises=[                                           │
  │  53 +                 f"hash={self.content_hash[:16]}...",             │
  │  54 +                 f"computed={computed[:16]}...",                  │
  │  55 +             ],                                                   │
  │  56 +             conclusion=f"valid={valid}"                          │
  │  57 +         )                                                        │
  │  58 +                                                                  │
  │  59 +     def proof(self) -> ProofObject:                              │
  │  60 +         return ProofObject(                                      │
  │  61 +             rule="Inode",                                        │
  │  62 +             premises=[                                           │
  │  63 +                 f"hash={self.content_hash[:16]}...",             │
  │  64 +                 f"type={self.inode_type.value}",                 │
  │  65 +                 f"size={int(self.size)}",                        │
  │  66 +             ],                                                   │
  │  67 +             conclusion="inode valid"                             │
  │  68 +         )                                                        │
  │  69 +                                                                  │
  │  70 +                                                                  │
  │  71 + @dataclass                                                       │
  │  72 + class ContentAddressedStorage:                                   │
  │  73 +     """Content-addressed storage backend."""                     │
  │  74 +     inodes: Dict[str, Inode] = field(default_factory=dict)       │
  │  75 +     content_store: Dict[str, bytes] = field(default_factory=dic… │
  │  76 +                                                                  │
  │  77 +     def store(                                                   │
  │  78 +         self,                                                    │
  │  79 +         content: bytes,                                          │
  │  80 +         inode_type: InodeType,                                   │
  │  81 +         owner: str,                                              │
  │  82 +         timestamp: str                                           │
  │  83 +     ) -> Tuple[Inode, ProofObject]:                              │
  │  84 +         """Store content and return inode."""                    │
  │  85 +         content_hash = hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()       │
  │  86 +                                                                  │
  │  87 +         # Check if already exists (deduplication)                │
  │  88 +         if content_hash in self.inodes:                          │
  │  89 +             return self.inodes[content_hash], ProofObject(       │
  │  90 +                 rule="CASStore",                                 │
  │  91 +                 premises=[f"hash={content_hash[:16]}..."],       │
  │  92 +                 conclusion="content already stored (dedup)"      │
  │  93 +             )                                                    │
  │  94 +                                                                  │
  │  95 +         inode = Inode(                                           │
  │  96 +             content_hash=content_hash,                           │
  │  97 +             inode_type=inode_type,                               │
  │  98 +             size=Fraction(len(content)),                         │
  │  99 +             owner=owner,                                         │
  │ 100 +             permissions=0o644,                                   │
  │ 101 +             created_at=timestamp,                                │
  │ 102 +             modified_at=timestamp,                               │
  │ 103 +             accessed_at=timestamp,                               │
  │ 104 +         )                                                        │
  │ 105 +                                                                  │
  │ 106 +         self.inodes[content_hash] = inode                        │
  │ 107 +         self.content_store[content_hash] = content               │
  │ 108 +                                                                  │
  │ 109 +         return inode, ProofObject(                               │
  │ 110 +             rule="CASStore",                                     │
  │ 111 +             premises=[f"hash={content_hash[:16]}...", f"size={l… │
  │ 112 +             conclusion="content stored"                          │
  │ 113 +         )                                                        │
  │ 114 +                                                                  │
  │ 115 +     def retrieve(self, content_hash: str) -> Tuple[Optional[byt… │
  │ 116 +         """Retrieve content by hash."""                          │
  │ 117 +         content = self.content_store.get(content_hash)           │
  │ 118 +                                                                  │
  │ 119 +         if content is None:                                      │
  │ 120 +             return None, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 121 +                 rule="CASRetrieve",                              │
  │ 122 +                 premises=[f"hash={content_hash[:16]}..."],       │
  │ 123 +                 conclusion="content not found"                   │
  │ 124 +             )                                                    │
  │ 125 +                                                                  │
  │ 126 +         return content, ProofObject(                             │
  │ 127 +             rule="CASRetrieve",                                  │
  │ 128 +             premises=[f"hash={content_hash[:16]}...", f"size={l… │
  │ 129 +             conclusion="content retrieved"                       │
  │ 130 +         )                                                        │

  │ 47 +         - Absolute vs relative paths                              │

  │ 72 +         result = PathResolution(                                  │

  │ 74 +             inode_hash=hash(resolved_path) & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF,… │
  │ 75 +             remaining_path="",                                    │
  │ 76 +             symlinks_followed=symlinks                            │
  │ 77 +         )                                                         │
  │ 78 +                                                                   │
  │ 79 +         proofs.append(result.proof())                             │
  │ 80 +                                                                   │
  │ 81 +         return result, proofs                                     │

  │ 102 +                                                                  │
  │ 103 +         # Check dependencies                                     │
  │ 104 +         for dep in service.dependencies:                         │
  │ 105 +             dep_state = self.service_states.get(dep.service, Se… │
  │ 106 +             if dep_state.value < dep.required_state.value:       │
  │ 107 +                 # Start dependency first                         │
  │ 108 +                 ok, dep_proofs = self.start_service(dep.service) │
  │ 109 +                 proofs.extend(dep_proofs)                        │
  │ 110 +                 if not ok:                                       │
  │ 111 +                     return False, proofs                         │
  │ 112 +                                                                  │
  │ 113 +         # Start the service                                      │
  │ 114 +         self.service_states[name] = ServiceState.RUNNING         │
  │ 115 +                                                                  │
  │ 116 +         proofs.append(ProofObject(                               │
  │ 117 +             rule="InitStart",                                    │
  │ 118 +             premises=[f"name={name}"],                           │
  │ 119 +             conclusion="service started"                         │
  │ 120 +         ))                                                       │
  │ 121 +                                                                  │
  │ 122 +         return True, proofs                                      │
  │ 123 +                                                                  │
  │ 124 +     def check_dependency_graph(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject… │
  │ 125 +         """Check that dependency graph has no cycles."""         │
  │ 126 +         # Build adjacency list                                   │
  │ 127 +         graph = {                                                │
  │ 128 +             name: [d.service for d in svc.dependencies]          │
  │ 129 +             for name, svc in self.services.items()               │
  │ 130 +         }                                                        │
  │ 131 +                                                                  │
  │ 132 +         # DFS for cycle detection                                │
  │ 133 +         visited = set()                                          │
  │ 134 +         rec_stack = set()                                        │
  │ 135 +                                                                  │
  │ 136 +         def has_cycle(node):                                     │
  │ 137 +             visited.add(node)                                    │
  │ 138 +             rec_stack.add(node)                                  │
  │ 139 +                                                                  │
  │ 140 +             for neighbor in graph.get(node, []):                 │
  │ 141 +                 if neighbor not in visited:                      │
  │ 142 +                     if has_cycle(neighbor):                      │
  │ 143 +                         return True                              │
  │ 144 +                 elif neighbor in rec_stack:                      │
  │ 145 +                     return True                                  │
  │ 146 +                                                                  │
  │ 147 +             rec_stack.remove(node)                               │
  │ 148 +             return False                                         │
  │ 149 +                                                                  │
  │ 150 +         for node in graph:                                       │
  │ 151 +             if node not in visited:                              │
  │ 152 +                 if has_cycle(node):                              │
  │ 153 +                     return False, ProofObject(                   │
  │ 154 +                         rule="InitCheckDeps",                    │
  │ 155 +                         premises=[],                             │
  │ 156 +                         conclusion="failed: cycle detected"      │
  │ 157 +                     )                                            │
  │ 158 +                                                                  │
  │ 159 +         return True, ProofObject(                                │
  │ 160 +             rule="InitCheckDeps",                                │
  │ 161 +             premises=[f"services={len(self.services)}"],         │
  │ 162 +             conclusion="dependency graph valid (DAG)"            │
  │ 163 +         )                                                        │

  │  95 +     """A stack frame for function calls."""                      │
  │  96 +     return_address: int                                          │
  │  97 +     locals: Dict[int, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)         │
  │  98 +     capability_context: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  │
  │  99 +                                                                  │
  │ 100 +                                                                  │
  │ 101 + @dataclass                                                       │
  │ 102 + class AbstractMachine:                                           │
  │ 103 +     """Abstract machine state."""                                │
  │ 104 +     code: List[Instruction] = field(default_factory=list)        │
  │ 105 +     stack: List[Any] = field(default_factory=list)               │
  │ 106 +     call_stack: List[StackFrame] = field(default_factory=list)   │
  │ 107 +     pc: int = 0  # Program counter                               │
  │ 108 +     memory: Dict[int, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)         │
  │ 109 +     halted: bool = False                                         │
  │ 110 +                                                                  │
  │ 111 +     def push(self, value: Any) -> None:                          │
  │ 112 +         """Push value onto stack."""                             │
  │ 113 +         self.stack.append(value)                                 │
  │ 114 +                                                                  │
  │ 115 +     def pop(self) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], ProofObject]:          │
  │ 116 +         """Pop value from stack."""                              │
  │ 117 +         if not self.stack:                                       │
  │ 118 +             return None, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 119 +                 rule="StackPop",                                 │
  │ 120 +                 premises=[],                                     │
  │ 121 +                 conclusion="failed: stack underflow"             │
  │ 122 +             )                                                    │
  │ 123 +                                                                  │
  │ 124 +         return self.stack.pop(), ProofObject(                    │
  │ 125 +             rule="StackPop",                                     │
  │ 126 +             premises=[f"depth={len(self.stack)}"],               │
  │ 127 +             conclusion="popped"                                  │
  │ 128 +         )                                                        │
  │ 129 +                                                                  │
  │ 130 +     def step(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                  │
  │ 131 +         """Execute one instruction."""                           │
  │ 132 +         if self.halted:                                          │
  │ 133 +             return False, ProofObject(                           │
  │ 134 +                 rule="AMStep",                                   │
  │ 135 +                 premises=["halted=true"],                        │
  │ 136 +                 conclusion="no step"                             │
  │ 137 +             )                                                    │
  │ 138 +                                                                  │
  │ 139 +         if self.pc >= len(self.code):                            │
  │ 140 +             self.halted = True                                   │
  │ 141 +             return False, ProofObject(                           │
  │ 142 +                 rule="AMStep",                                   │
  │ 143 +                 premises=["pc>=len(code)"],                      │
  │ 144 +                 conclusion="halted (end of code)"                │
  │ 145 +             )                                                    │
  │ 146 +                                                                  │
  │ 147 +         instr = self.code[self.pc]                               │
  │ 148 +                                                                  │
  │ 149 +         # Execute instruction (simplified)                       │
  │ 150 +         if instr.opcode == OpCode.HALT:                          │
  │ 151 +             self.halted = True                                   │
  │ 152 +         elif instr.opcode == OpCode.PUSH:                        │
  │ 153 +             self.push(instr.operand)                             │
  │ 154 +             self.pc += 1                                         │
  │ 155 +         elif instr.opcode == OpCode.POP:                         │
  │ 156 +             _, _ = self.pop()                                    │
  │ 157 +             self.pc += 1                                         │
  │ 158 +         elif instr.opcode == OpCode.ADD:                         │
  │ 159 +             b, _ = self.pop()                                    │
  │ 160 +             a, _ = self.pop()                                    │
  │ 161 +             if a is not None and b is not None:                  │
  │ 162 +                 self.push(a + b)                                 │
  │ 163 +             self.pc += 1                                         │
  │ 164 +         else:                                                    │
  │ 165 +             self.pc += 1                                         │
  │ 166 +                                                                  │
  │ 167 +         return True, ProofObject(                                │
  │ 168 +             rule="AMStep",                                       │
  │ 169 +             premises=[f"pc={self.pc}", f"opcode={instr.opcode.n… │
  │ 170 +             conclusion="step executed"                           │
  │ 171 +         )                                                        │
  │ 172 +                                                                  │
  │ 173 +     def run(self) -> Tuple[Any, ProofObject]:                    │
  │ 174 +         """Run until halt."""                                    │
  │ 175 +         steps = 0                                                │
  │ 176 +         max_steps = 10000  # Prevent infinite loops              │
  │ 177 +                                                                  │
  │ 178 +         while not self.halted and steps < max_steps:             │
  │ 179 +             self.step()                                          │
  │ 180 +             steps += 1                                           │
  │ 181 +                                                                  │
  │ 182 +         result = self.stack[-1] if self.stack else None          │
  │ 183 +                                                                  │
  │ 184 +         return result, ProofObject(                              │
  │ 185 +             rule="AMRun",                                        │
  │ 186 +             premises=[f"steps={steps}", f"halted={self.halted}"… │
  │ 187 +             conclusion="execution complete"                      │
  │ 188 +         )                                                        │
  ╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯

  │ 415 +     """Directed Acyclic Graph — a category without cycles."""    │
  │ 416 +     nodes: Set[str]                                              │
  │ 417 +     edges: Dict[str, Set[str]]  # node -> set of children        │
  │ 418 +                                                                  │
  │ 419 +     def has_cycle(self) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:             │
  │ 420 +         """Check if graph has a cycle using DFS."""              │
  │ 421 +         visited = set()                                          │
  │ 422 +         rec_stack = set()                                        │
  │ 423 +                                                                  │
  │ 424 +         def dfs(node):                                           │
  │ 425 +             visited.add(node)                                    │
  │ 426 +             rec_stack.add(node)                                  │
  │ 427 +                                                                  │
  │ 428 +             for neighbor in self.edges.get(node, set()):         │
  │ 429 +                 if neighbor not in visited:                      │
  │ 430 +                     if dfs(neighbor):                            │
  │ 431 +                         return True                              │
  │ 432 +                 elif neighbor in rec_stack:                      │
  │ 433 +                     return True                                  │
  │ 434 +                                                                  │
  │ 435 +             rec_stack.remove(node)                               │
  │ 436 +             return False                                         │
  │ 437 +                                                                  │
  │ 438 +         for node in self.nodes:                                  │
  │ 439 +             if node not in visited:                              │
  │ 440 +                 if dfs(node):                                    │
  │ 441 +                     return True, ProofObject(                    │
  │ 442 +                         rule="DAGCycleCheck",                    │
  │ 443 +                         premises=[],                             │
  │ 444 +                         conclusion="cycle detected"              │
  │ 445 +                     )                                            │
  │ 446 +                                                                  │
  │ 447 +         return False, ProofObject(                               │
  │ 448 +             rule="DAGCycleCheck",                                │
  │ 449 +             premises=[f"nodes={len(self.nodes)}"],               │
  │ 450 +             conclusion="no cycles (valid DAG)"                   │
  │ 451 +         )                                                        │
  │ 452                                                                    │
  │ 453                                                                    │
  │ 454   @dataclass                                                       │
